return \"tie\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef parse_factor_suggestions(payload: Any) -> list[dict[str, str]]:\n    rows: list = []\n    if isinstance(payload, dict):\n        raw = payload.get(\"factors\") \n122: def historical_stats(prior: list[PairAnswer], a: int, b: int) -> dict[str, int]:\n123:     count = 0\n124:     wins_a = 0\n125:     wins_b = 0\n126:     key = pair_key(a, b)\n127:     for p in return True\n202: \n203: \n204: def acquisition_score(\n205:     state: SchedulerState,\n206:     pair: tuple[int, int],\n207:     scores: dict[int, float],\n208:     ranks: dict[int, int],\n209: ) -> float:\n210:     a, b = pair\n211:     sa = scores.get(a, 0.0)\n212:     sb = scores.get(b, 0.0)\n213:     p = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-(sa - sb)))\n214:     info = 4.0 * p * (1.0 - p)\n215:     ra = item_id in items) / len(items)\n        for item_id in items:\n            scores[item_id] = scores.get(item_id, 0.0) - mean\n    return scores\n\n\ndef ranks_from_scores(items: list[int], scores: dict[int, float]) -> dict[int, int]:\n    ordered = sorted(items, key=lambda i: (-(scores.get(i, 0.0)), i))\n    return {item_id: idx + 1 for idx, item_id in enumerate(ordered)}\n\n\ndef components(items: list[int], edges: list[tuple[int, int]]) -> list[list[int]]:\n    adj: dict[int, list[int]] = {int(i): [] for i in items}\n    for a, b in edges:\n        if a in adj:\n            adj[a].append(b)\n        if b in adj:\n            adj[b].append(a)\n    seen: set[int] = set()\n    out: list[list[int]] = []\n    for item_id in items:\n        if item_id in seen:\n            continue\n        stack = [item_id]\n        comp: list[int] = []\n        seen.add(item_id)\n        while stack:\n            cur = stack.pop()\n            comp.append(cur)\n            for nxt in adj.get(cur, []):\n                if nxt not in seen:\n                    seen.add(nxt)\n                    stack.append(nxt)\n        out.append(comp)\n    return out\n\n\ndef used_pair_set(answers: list[PairAnswer]) -> set[str]:\n    return {pair_key(a.item_a, a.item_b) for a in answers}\n\n\ndef historical_stats(prior: list[PairAnswer], a: int, b: int) -> dict[str, int]:\n    count = 0\n    wins_a = 0\n    wins_b = 0\n    key = pair_key(a, b)\n    for p in prior:\n        if pair_key(p.item_a, p.item_b) != key:\n            continue\n        count += 1\n        if p.response != \"winner\" or p.winner_id is None:\n            continue\n        if p.winner_id == a:\n            wins_a += 1\n        elif p.winner_id == b:\n            wins_b += 1\n    return {\"count\": count, \"winsA\": wins_a, \"winsB\": wins_b}\n\n\ndef enumerate_legal_pairs(state: SchedulerState) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:\n    used = used_pair_set(state.answers)\n    last = state.last_pair\n    all_pairs: list[tuple[int, int]] = []\n    disjoint: list[tuple[int, int]] = []\n    n = len(state.items)\n    for i in range(n):\n        for j in range(i + 1, n):\n            a = state.items[i]\n            b = state.items[j]\n            if pair_key(a, b) in used:\n                continue\n            all_pairs.append((a, b))\n            if not last or (a != last[0] and a != last[1] and b != last[0] and b != last[1]):\n                disjoint.append((a, b))\n    return disjoint if disjoint else all_pairs\n\n\ndef unseen_items(state: SchedulerState) -> list[int]:\n    seen: set[int] = set()\n    for a in state.answers:\n        seen.add(a.item_a)\n        seen.add(a.item_b)\n    return [item_id for item_id in state.items if item_id not in seen]\n\n\ndef remains_feasible(state: SchedulerState, pair: tuple[int, int]) -> bool:\n    remaining_after = state.question_budget - len(state.answers) - 1\n    if remaining_after <= 0:\n        return True\n    next_state = SchedulerState(\n        items=state.items,\n        target=state.target,\n        pass_index=state.pass_index,\n        question_budget=state.question_budget,\n        answers=list(state.answers) + [\n            PairAnswer(\n                item_a=pair[0],\n                item_b=pair[1],\n                winner_id=pair[0],\n                response=\"winner\",\n                presented_left_id=pair[0],\n                presented_right_id=pair[1],\n                decision_seconds=0,\n            )\n        ],\n        prior_answers=state.prior_answers,\n        last_pair=pair,\n    )\n    legal = enumerate_legal_pairs(next_state)\n    if not legal:\n        return False\n    if state.pass_index == 1:\n        unseen = unseen_items(next_state)\n        if len(unseen) > remaining_after * 2:\n            return False\n        if len(unseen) == 1:\n            u = unseen[0]\n            can_cover = any(a == u or b == u for a, b in legal)\n            if not can_cover and remaining_after == 1:\n                return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef acquisition_score(\n    state: SchedulerState,\n    pair: tuple[int, int],\n    scores: dict[int, float],\n    ranks: dict[int, int],\n) -> float:\n    a, b = pair\n    sa = scores.get(a, 0.0)\n    sb = scores.get(b, 0.0)\n    p = 1.0 / (1.0 + math.exp(-(sa - sb)))\n    info = 4.0 * p * (1.0 - p)\n    ra = ranks.get(a, 1)\n    rb = ranks.get(b, 1)\n    n = len(state.items)\n    top_n = max(1, min(state.target.n or 1, math.floor(n / 2) or 1))\n    t = 1.0\n    if state.target.mode == \"winner\":\n        ta = math.exp(-(ra - 1) / 2)\n        tb = math.exp(-(rb - 1) / 2)\n        t = (ta + tb) / 2\n        if ra == 1 or rb == 1:\n            t += 0.35\n    elif state.target.mode == \"top_n\":\n        ordered = sorted(state.items, key=lambda x: ranks.get(x, 0))\n        if len(ordered) > top_n:\n            boundary = (scores.get(ordered[top_n - 1], 0.0) + scores.get(ordered[top_n], 0.0)) / 2\n        else:\n            boundary = scores.get(ordered[-1], 0.0) if ordered else 0.0\n        da = math.exp(-abs(sa - boundary) / 0.75)\n        db = math.exp(-abs(sb - boundary) / 0.75)\n        t = (da + db) / 2\n        straddles = (ra <= top_n) != (rb <= top_n)\n        if straddles:\n            t += 0.55\n    else:\n        t = math.exp(-(abs(ra - rb) - 1) / 2.5)\n    hist = historical_stats(state.prior_answers, a, b)\n    r = 1.0 / math.sqrt(1 + 0.65 * hist[\"count\"])\n    exposure = {int(i): 0 for i in state.items}\n    for ans in state.answers:\n        exposure[ans.item_a] = exposure.get(ans.item_a, 0) + 1\n        exposure[ans.item_b] = exposure.get(ans.item_b, 0) + 1\n    e = 1 + 0.15 * (2 - min(exposure.get(a, 0), 2) - min(exposure.get(b, 0), 2))\n    edges: list[tuple[int, int]] = []\n    for ans in [*state.prior_answers, *state.answers]:\n        if ans.response in (\"unsure\", \"skipped\"):\n            continue\n        edges.append((ans.item_a, ans.item_b))\n    comps = components(state.items, edges)\n    in_same = any(a in c and b in c for c in comps)\n    g = 1.0 if in_same else 2.4\n    bonus = 0.0\n    if hist[\"count\"] >= 2 and hist[\"winsA\"] and hist[\"winsB\"]:\n        bonus += 0.25\n    return info * t * r * e * g + bonus\n\n\ndef select_next_pair(state: SchedulerState) -> tuple[int, int] | None:\n    if len(state.answers) >= state.question_budget:\n        return None\n    if len(state.items) < 2:\n        return None\n    pair_cap = max_unique_pairs(len(state.items))\n    if len(state.answers) >= pair_cap:\n        return None\n    candidates = enumerate_legal_pairs(state)\n    if not candidates:\n        return None\n    require_coverage = state.pass_index == 1\n    unseen = unseen_items(state)\n    if require_coverage and len(unseen) >= 2:\n        unseen_set = set(unseen)\n        both_unseen = [(a, b) for a, b in candidates if a in unseen_set and b in unseen_set]\n        if both_unseen:\n            candidates = both_unseen\n    elif require_coverage and len(unseen) == 1:\n        u = unseen[0]\n        cover = [(a, b) for a, b in candidates if a == u or b == u]\n        if cover:\n            candidates = cover\n    feasible = [pair for pair in candidates if remains_feasible(state, pair)]\n    pool = feasible if feasible else candidates\n    all_answers = [*state.prior_answers, *state.answers]\n    scores = fit_provisional_scores(state.items, all_answers)\n    ranks = ranks_from_scores(state.items, scores)\n    best = pool[0]\n    best_score = float(\"-inf\")\n    seed = hash_seed([state.pass_index, state.question_budget, *state.items])\n    for i, pair in enumerate(pool):\n        s = acquisition_score(state, pair, scores, ranks)\n        s += seeded_unit(seed + i) * 1e-6\n        if s > best_score:\n            best_score = s\n            best = pair\n    return best\n\n\ndef choose_orientation(\n    a: int,\n    b: int,\n    history: list[PairAnswer],\n    seed_parts: list[str | int],\n) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    left_a = 0\n    left_b = 0\n    for h in history:\n        if h.presented_left_id == a:\n            left_a += 1\n        if h.presented_left_id == b:\n            left_b += 1\n    if left_a < left_b:\n        return a, b\n    if left_b < left_a:\n        return b, a\n    if seeded_unit(hash_seed([*seed_parts, a, b])) < 0.5:\n        return a, b\n    return b, a\n\n\ndef rank_order_from_answers(items: list[int], answers: list[PairAnswer]) -> list[int]:\n    scores = fit_provisional_scores(items, answers)\n    return sorted(items, key=lambda i: (-(scores.get(i, 0.0)), i))\n\n\ndef suggested_budget(n: int) -> int:\n    return ford_johnson_budget(n)\n\n\ndef pairing_to_answer(p: dict) -> PairAnswer | None:\n    try:\n        left_raw = p.get(\"presented_left_id\", p.get(\"presentedLeftId\", p.get(\"item_a_id\", p.get(\"winner_id\"))))\n        right_raw = p.get(\"presented_right_id\", p.get(\"presentedRightId\", p.get(\"item_b_id\", p.get(\"loser_id\"))))\n        a_raw = p.get(\"item_a_id\", p.get(\"winner_id\", left_raw))\n        b_raw = p.get(\"item_b_id\", p.get(\"loser_id\", right_raw))\n        a = int(a_raw)\n        b = int(b_raw)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return None\n    if a == b:\n        return None\n    try:\n        left = int(left_raw) if left_raw is not None else a\n        right = int(right_raw) if right_raw is not None else b\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        left, right = a, b\n    response = str(p.get(\"response\") or \"winner\")\n    winner_raw = p.get(\"winner_id\")\n    try:\n        winner = int(winner_raw) if winner_raw is not None else (a if response == \"winner\" else None)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        winner = a if response == \"winner\" else None\n    try:\n        seconds = float(p.get(\"decision_seconds\", p.get(\"decisionSeconds\", 0)) or 0)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        seconds = 0.0\n    return PairAnswer(\n        item_a=a,\n        item_b=b,\n        winner_id=winner,\n        response=response,\n        presented_left_id=left,\n        presented_right_id=right,\n        decision_seconds=seconds,\n    )\n\n\ndef pairings_to_answers(rows: list | None) -> list[PairAnswer]:\n    out: list[PairAnswer] = []\n    for p in rows or []:\n        if not isinstance(p, dict):\n            continue\n        ans = pairing_to_answer(p)\n        if ans is not None:\n            out.append(ans)\n    return out\n\n\ndef answer_to_pairing(ans: PairAnswer) -> dict:\n    loser = ans.item_b if ans.winner_id == ans.item_a else ans.item_a\n    if ans.response != \"winner\" or ans.winner_id is None:\n        winner = ans.presented_left_id\n        loser = ans.presented_right_id\n    else:\n        winner = ans.winner_id\n        loser = ans.item_b if winner == ans.item_a else ans.item_a\n        if loser == winner:\n            loser = ans.presented_right_id if winner == ans.presented_left_id else ans.presented_left_id\n    return {\n        \"winner_id\": int(winner),\n        \"loser_id\": int(loser),\n        \"response\": str(ans.response),\n        \"decision_seconds\": float(ans.decision_seconds or 0.0),\n        \"presented_left_id\": int(ans.presented_left_id),\n        \"presented_right_id\": int(ans.presented_right_id),\n        \"item_a_id\": int(ans.item_a),\n        \"item_b_id\": int(ans.item_b),\n        \"effective_rule\": \"choice\",\n    }\n\n\ndef ranking_target_from_group(group: dict) -> RankingTarget:\n    mode = str(group.get(\"ranking_target\") or \"full\").strip().lower()\n    if mode in (\"winner\", \"find_best\"):\n        return RankingTarget(mode=\"winner\", n=1)\n    if mode in (\"top_n\", \"find_top_3\", \"find_top_half\"):\n        n = group.get(\"top_n\")\n        try:\n            top_n = int(n) if n is not None else 1\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            top_n = 1\n        return RankingTarget(mode=\"top_n\", n=max(1, top_n))\n    return RankingTarget(mode=\"full\")","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":415,"totalLines":415,"truncated":false}},"title":"src/utils/pair_scheduler.py","time":{"start":1787339338545,"end":1787339339049}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_01a025b9f09670019259217e8b0f4829"}},"id":"prt_025b9f1c60010ZohJz8JKwD1wJ","sessionID":"ses_fda463433ffe05Mbp697Cym8jx","messageID":"msg_025b9eb890016BbGZo94cf08Bv"}}

\"error\": None, \"provider\": provider}\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(\"src.pvf.api.app_context_views.verify_llm_provider\", _fake_verify)\n    with _ai_enabled(monkeypatch):\n        res = model_key=model_key,\n        status=status,\n        started_date=datetime.now() if status == \"in_progress\" else None,\n    )\n    session.add(row)\n    session.commit()\n    session.refresh(row)\n    if modify_date is not None:\n        row.modify_date = modify_date\n        session.add(row)\n        session.commit()\n        session.refresh(row)\n    job_id = int(row.id)\n    session.close()\n    return job_id\n\n\ndef test_claim_next_fills_queued_under_cap(account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Claim fill\")\n    queued_id = _insert_job(project, status=\"queued\", model_key=\"q1\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(AiAgentJob, \"count_in_progress\", staticmethod(lambda session, clear_lock=False: 0))\n    session = get_next_session()\n    claimed = AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent=2, clear_lock=False)\n    claimed_id = claimed.id if claimed is not None else None\n    claimed_status = claimed.status if claimed is not None else None\n    started = claimed.started_date if claimed is not None else None\n    session.close()\n    assert claimed_id == queued_id\n    assert claimed_status == \"in_progress\"\n    assert started is not None\n\n\ndef test_claim_next_at_cap_does_not_start_queued(account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, title=\"Claim cap\")\n    queued_id = _insert_job(project, status=\"queued\", model_key=\"q-cap\")\n    monkeypatch.setattr(AiAgentJob, \"count_in_progress\", staticmethod(lambda session, clear_lock=False: 2))\n    session = get_next_session()\n    claimed =      assert claimed_id != queued_id\n        assert claimed_status == \"in_progress\"\n\n\ndef test_claim_next_round_robins_in_progress(account, monkeypatch):\n    project = create_project(account, model_key=\"new\", modify_date=datetime(2000, 1, 2))\n    monkeypatch.setattr(AiAgentJob, \"count_in_progress\", staticmethod(lambda session, clear_lock=False: 2))\n    session = get_next_session()\n    first = AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent=2, clear_lock=False)\n    first_id = first.id if first is not None else None\n    second = AiAgentJob.claim_next(session, max_concurrent=2, clear_lock=False)\n    second_id = second.id if second is not None else None\n    session.close()\n    assert first_id == older_id\n    assert second_id == newer_id\n\n\ndef    if v is None:\n        return None\n    try:\n        return int(v)\n    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n        return None\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass PairObs:\n    participant_id: int\n    channel: str\n    factor_id: int | None\n    winner_id: int\n    loser_id: int\n    y: float\n    left_sign: float\n    group_key: str\n    seq: int\n    response: str\n    legacy: bool\n\n\ndef _group_key(g: dict, fallback: int) -> str:\n    for k in (\"id\", \"group_token\", \"client_group_id\"):\n        v = g.get(k)\n        if v is not None and str(v):\n            return str(v)\n    return f\"anon:{fallback}\"\n\n\ndef _left_sign(winner_id: int, loser_id: int, pairing: dict) -> float:\n    left = pairing.get(\"presented_left_id\")\n    if left is None:\n        left = pairing.get(\"presentedLeftId\")\n    lid = _int_or_none(left)\n    if lid is None:\n        return 0.0\n    if lid == int(winner_id):\n        return 1.0\n    if lid == int(loser_id):\n        return -1.0\n    return 0.0\n\n\ndef _usable_pairing(raw: Any) -> PairObs | None:\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        return None\n    resp = raw.get(\"response\") or \"winner\"\n    if hasattr(resp, \"value\"):\n        resp = resp.value\n    resp = str(resp).lower()\n    if resp in SOFT_EXCLUDE:\n        return None\n    if resp not in USABLE:\n        if raw.get(\"winner_id\") is not None and raw.get(\"loser_id\") is not None:\n            resp = \"winner\"\n        else:\n            return None\n    try:\n        w = int(raw[\"winner_id\"])\n        l = int(raw[\"loser_id\"])\n    except (TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):\n        return None\n    if w == l:\n        return None\n    y = 0.5 if resp == \"tie\" else 1.0\n    return PairObs(\n        participant_id=0,\n        channel=\"\",\n        factor_id=None,\n        winner_id=w,\n        loser_id=l,\n        y=y,\n        left_sign=_left_sign(w, l, raw),\n        group_key=\"\",\n        seq=0,\n        response=resp,\n        legacy=False,\n    )\n\n\ndef _adjacent_from_rank_order(order: list) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:\n    ids: list[int] = []\n    for x in order or []:\n        try:\n            ids.append(int(x))\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            continue\n    out = []\n    for a, b in zip(ids, ids[1:]):\n        if a != b:\n            out.append((a, b))\n    return out\n\n\ndef extract_observations(groups: list | None) -> list[PairObs]:\n    \"\"\"All answered pairings, including in-progress. Soft answers excluded.\n\n    Groups with empty pairings fall back to adjacent rank_order winner pairs.\n    \"\"\"\n    out: list[PairObs] = []\n    for gi, raw in enumerate(groups or []):\n        g = _as_dict(raw)\n        pid = int(g.get(\"participant_id\") or 0)\n        gt = _group_type(g)\n        channel = \"factor\" if gt == \"criteria\" else \"option\"\n        cid = g.get(\"criterion_id\")\n        factor_id = None if cid is None else _int_or_none(cid)\n        gkey = _group_key(g, gi)\n        pairings = g.get(\"pairings\") or []\n        used_legacy = False\n        parsed: list[PairObs] = []\n        if isinstance(pairings, list) and pairings:\n            for seq, p in enumerate(pairings):\n                obs = _usable_pairing(p)\n                if obs is None:\n                    continue\n                parsed.append(obs)\n        if not parsed:\n            for seq, (w, l) in enumerate(_adjacent_from_rank_order(g.get(\"rank_order\") or [])):\n   loser_id=l,\n                    y=1.0,\n                    left_sign=0.0,\n                    group_key=\"\",\n                    seq=seq,\n                    response=\"winner\",\n                    legacy=True,\n                ))\n                used_legacy = True\n        for seq, obs in enumerate(parsed):\n            out.append(PairObs(\n                participant_id=pid,\n                channel=channel,\n                factor_id=factor_id,\n                winner_id=obs.winner_id,\n                loser_id=obs.loser_id,\n                y=obs.y,\n                left_sign=obs.left_sign,\n                group_key=gkey,\n                seq=seq,\n                response=obs.response,\n                legacy=used_legacy or obs.legacy,\n            ))\n    return out\n\n\ndef fingerprint_observations(obs: list[PairObs]) -> str:\n    import hashlib\n\n    parts = [\n        f\"{o.participant_id}:{o.channel}:{o.factor_id}:{o.group_key}:{o.seq}:\"\n        f\"{o.winner_id}:{o.loser_id}:{o.y}:{o.response}\"\n        for o in obs\n    ]\n    parts.sort()\n    return hashlib.sha256(\"|\".join(parts).encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n\n\ndef The goal is to ensure agents make a best-effort choice while preserving abstention only below the threshold.\\n\\n* Scope: threshold math (`ceil(group_size * 0.15)` on combined skip+tie), prompt template assembly/removal of options, and default prompt copy — no toteboard/board storage or config changes.\\n* Verify logic: where group size is sourced, how combined value is computed/rounded, and that prompt mutation actually omits SKIP/TIE after threshold (including unit/mocked-vote reproduction).\\n* Diagnose 100% SKIP bias: examine prompt framing, option ordering, instructions and temperature that may make SKIP the safe default.\\n* Refine prompt to encourage decisiveness (e.g., add \\\"Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user\\\") and consider stronger nudges like ranked-choice or \\\"avoid SKIP unless insufficient information.\\\"\\n* Open questions: remove SKIP and TIE jointly vs independently, correct handling for small groups where ceil(15%) =1, and test strategy without live LLM calls (snapshot prompt output + simulated vote tallies).\",\n\n  Line 5467:           \"expandedDescription\": \"I'll look up how group size settings and FJ defaults work so the expansion matches the codebase.Checking how group size and FJ defaults are stored so the expansion is accurate.Group size (`option_questions_per_group` / `factor_questions_per_group`) must track whether the user overrode the Ford–Johnson default, not just store a number. Today those fields are always persisted as ints and reclamped, so adding items cannot tell “still on default” from “user chose this budget.” If the value was never explicitly set, new items should retarget the current FJ default for the new `n`; if it was set, keep the user’s value (still clamp to `question_budget_bounds`).\\n\\n- Scope: persist an explicit/default flag (or nullable until first edit) for option and factor group size; on item add/remove, apply FJ default only when not explicit.\\n- Recompute using `default_questions_per_group` / `ford_johnson_budget(n)` and existing clamp helpers; do not rewrite explicit values except to stay in bounds.\\n- Cover create/update APIs, vote session partitioning, and the project settings UI so the control can show default vs custom.\\n- Consider: treat matching the current FJ number as still-default, or only as default until the user touches the control?\\n- Open: should item deletion also retarget defaults? \"\".join(secrets.choice(GROUP_TOKEN_ALPHABET) for _ in range(length))\n\n\ndef normalize_ranking_mode(mode: Any, *, exclusive_mode: bool | None = None) -> str:\n    raw = mode.value if hasattr(mode, \"value\") else mode\n    m = str(raw or \"\").strip().lower().replace(\"-\", \"_\").replace(\" \", \"_\")\n    aliases = {\n        \"winner\": \"find_best\",\n        \"pick_one\": \"find_best\",\n        \"exclusive\": \"find_best\",\n        \"top_3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"top3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"findtop3\": \"find_top_3\",\n        \"top_half\": \"find_top_half\",\n        \"findtophalf\": \"find_top_half\",\n        \"full\": \"rank_all\",\n        \"rank\": \"rank_all\",\n        \"rankall\": \"rank_all\",\n    }\n    m = aliases.get(m, m)\n    if m in RANKING_MODES:\n        return m\n    if exclusive_mode:\n        return \"find_best\"\n    return \"rank_all\"\n\n\ndef exclusive_from_ranking_mode(mode: Any) -> bool:\n    return normalize_ranking_mode(mode) == \"find_best\"\n\n\ndef resolve_ranking_target(\n    project: Any,\n    *,\n    n_items: int,\n    group_type: str,\n) -> tuple[str, int | None]:\n    if str(group_type) == \"criteria\":\n        return \"full\", None\n    mode = normalize_ranking_mode(\n        getattr(project, \"ranking_mode\", None),\n        exclusive_mode=bool(getattr(project, \"project_exclusive_mode\", False)),\n    )\n    n = max(0, int(n_items or 0))\n    if mode == \"find_best\":\n        return \"winner\", 1\n    if mode == \"find_top_3\":\n        return \"top_n\", min(3, max(1, n // 2)) if n else 1\n    if mode == \"find_top_half\":\n        return \"top_n\", max(1, n // 2) if n else 1\n    return \"full\", None\n\n\ndef project_ranking_mode(project: Any) -> str:\n    return normalize_ranking_mode(\n        getattr(project, \"ranking_mode\", None),\n        exclusive_mode=bool(getattr(project, \"project_exclusive_mode\", False)),\n    )\n\n\ndef project_pass_settings(project: Any) -> dict:\n    mn = clamp_min_expected_passes(getattr(project, \"min_expected_passes\", None))\n    mx = clamp_max_recommended_passes(getattr(project, \"max_recommended_passes\", None), mn)\n    return {\n        \"min_expected_passes\": mn,\n        \"max_recommended_passes\": mx,\n        \"hard_max_passes\": hard_max_passes(mx, mn),\n        \"option_questions_per_group\": clamp_questions_per_group_stored(\n            getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group\", None)\n        ),\n        \"factor_questions_per_group\": clamp_questions_per_group_stored(\n            getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group\", None)\n        ),\n        \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\": bool(\n            getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)\n        ),\n        \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\": bool(\n            getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)\n        ),\n        \"ranking_mode\": project_ranking_mode(project),\n    }\n\n\ndef resolved_question_budgets(project: Any, option_n: int, factor_n: int) -> tuple[int, int]:\n    return (\n        resolve_questions_per_group(\n            getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group\", None),\n            option_n,\n            bool(getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)),\n        ),\n        resolve_questions_per_group(\n            getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group\", None),\n            factor_n,\n            bool(getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)),\n        ),\n    )\n\n\ndef _is_complete_group(g: dict) -> bool:\n    status = g.get(\"status\")\n    if hasattr(status, \"value\"):\n        status = status.value\n    if status in (None, \"\"):\n        return True\n    return str(status) == \"complete\"\n\n\ndef _complete_group_dicts(groups: list | None) -> list[dict]:\n    return [g for g in _group_dicts(groups) if _is_complete_group(g)]\n\n\ndef _in_progress_group_dicts(groups: list | None) -> list[dict]:\n    out = []\n    for g in _group_dicts(groups):\n        status = g.get(\"status\")\n        if hasattr(status, \"value\"):\n            status = status.value\n        if str(status or \"\") == \"in_progress\":\n            out.append(g)\n    return out\n\n\ndef partition_item_ids(item_ids: list[int], question_budget: int, rng: random.Random) -> list[list[int]]:\n    \"\"\"One group per channel per pass: a random subset up to the budget's item bound.\n\n    FJ(n) is informational only — a group never splits a factor into batches;\n    coverage comes from repeated passes over different random subsets.\n    \"\"\"\n    ids = [int(i) for i in item_ids]\n    if len(ids) < 2:\n        return []\n    budget = max(1, int(question_budget or 1))\n    shuffled = list(ids)\n    rng.shuffle(shuffled)\n    k = max_items_for_question_budget(budget)\n    return [shuffled[:k]] if len(shuffled) > k else [shuffled]\n\n\ndef option_batch_count(option_n: int, question_budget: int) -> int:\n    return 1 if option_n >= 2 else 0\n\n\ndef _group_key(group_type: str, criterion_id: int | None, pass_index: int, batch_index: int = 0) -> str:\n    c = criterion_id if criterion_id is not None else OVERALL_CRITERION_ID\n    return f\"{pass_index}:{group_type}:{c}:{int(batch_index or 0)}\"\n\n\ndef _group_dicts(groups: list | None) -> list[dict]:\n    return [_as_dict(g) for g in (groups or [])]\n\n\ndef max_completed_pass(groups: list | None) -> int:\n    done = _complete_group_dicts(groups)\n    if not done:\n        return 0\n    return max(int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) for g in done)\n\n\ndef completed_keys_for_pass(groups: list | None, pass_index: int) -> set[str]:\n    keys: set[str] = set()\n    for g in _complete_group_dicts(groups):\n        if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) != pass_index:\n            continue\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        cid = g.get(\"criterion_id\")\n        keys.add(_group_key(str(gt), cid, pass_index, int(g.get(\"batch_index\") or 0)))\n    return keys\n\n\ndef _group_item_ids(g: dict) -> set[int]:\n    raw = g.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or g.get(\"item_ids\") or g.get(\"rank_order\") or []\n    return {int(x) for x in raw}\n\n\ndef channel_covered_item_ids(\n    groups: list | None,\n    *,\n    pass_index: int,\n    group_type: str,\n    criterion_id: int | None,\n) -> set[int]:\n    covered: set[int] = set()\n    for g in _complete_group_dicts(groups):\n        if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) != pass_index:\n            continue\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        if str(gt) != str(group_type):\n            continue\n        if g.get(\"criterion_id\") != criterion_id:\n            continue\n        covered |= _group_item_ids(g)\n    return covered\n\n\ndef required_slots_for_pass(\n    *,\n    pass_index: int,\n    alternative_ids: list[int],\n    factor_ids: list[int],\n    option_question_budget: int | None = None,\n    factor_question_budget: int | None = None,\n    seed: Any = None,\n    rng: random.Random | None = None,\n) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Option groups first (shuffled by caller), then factor group if needed.\n\n    When ``seed`` is provided the per-channel item shuffle is deterministic for\n    that seed, so repeated calls produce identical batches and completed-group\n    coverage checks match across issuances.\n    \"\"\"\n    slots: list[dict] = []\n    opt_budget = int(option_question_budget or clamp_questions_per_group_stored(None))\n    fac_budget = int(factor_question_budget or clamp_questions_per_group_stored(None))\n    channels: list[tuple[str, int | None, list[int], int]] = []\n    if factor_ids:\n        for fid in factor_ids:\n            channels.append((\"alternative\", int(fid), list(alternative_ids), opt_budget))\n    else:\n        channels.append((\"alternative\", None, list(alternative_ids), opt_budget))\n    if len(factor_ids) >= 2:\n        channels.append((\"criteria\", None, list(factor_ids), fac_budget))\n    for group_type, criterion_id, item_ids, budget in channels:\n        channel_rng = (\n            random.Random(f\"{seed}:{pass_index}:{group_type}:{criterion_id}\")\n            if seed is not None\n            else (rng or random.Random())\n        )\n        n = len(item_ids)\n        if group_type == \"criteria\":\n            # The factor list itself is the target; never truncate or split it.\n            if n >= 2:\n                slots.append({\n                    \"group_type\": group_type,\n                    \"criterion_id\": criterion_id,\n                    \"pass_index\": pass_index,\n                    \"batch_index\": 0,\n                    \"item_ids\": list(item_ids),\n                    \"question_budget\": clamp_questions_per_group(budget, n),\n                })\n            continue\n        q = clamp_questions_per_group(budget, n) if n >= 2 else 0\n        batches = partition_item_ids(item_ids, q or 1, channel_rng) if n >= 2 else []\n        for batch_i, batch_ids in enumerate(batches):\n            slots.append({\n                \"group_type\": group_type,\n                \"criterion_id\": criterion_id,\n                \"pass_index\": pass_index,\n                \"batch_index\": batch_i,\n                \"item_ids\": list(batch_ids),\n                \"question_budget\": clamp_questions_per_group(budget, len(batch_ids)),\n            })\n    return slots\n\n\ndef mean_ranks_from_order(rank_order: list[int]) -> dict[int, float]:\n    return {int(item_id): float(i) for i, item_id in enumerate(rank_order)}\n\n\ndef participant_option_ranks_for_pass(\n    groups: list | None,\n    *,\n    pass_index: int,\n    option_ids: list[int],\n) -> dict[int, float]:\n    \"\"\"Mean rank across option-sort groups in a pass (lower better).\"\"\"\n    acc: dict[int, list[float]] = {int(i): [] for i in option_ids}\n    for g in _group_dicts(groups):\n        if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) != pass_index:\n            continue\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        if str(gt) != \"alternative\":\n            continue\n        from .sort_compare import effective_group_rank_order\n        order = effective_group_rank_order(g)\n        ranks = mean_ranks_from_order(order)\n        for oid, r in ranks.items():\n            if oid in acc:\n                acc[oid].append(r)\n    out: dict[int, float] = {}\n    for oid, vals in acc.items():\n        if vals:\n            out[oid] = sum(vals) / len(vals)\n        else:\n            out[oid] = float(len(option_ids))  # unknown → worst\n    return out\n\n\ndef excluded_option_ids(\n    *,\n    exclusive_mode: bool,\n    all_option_ids: list[int],\n    groups: list | None,\n    before_pass: int | None = None,\n) -> set[int]:\n    \"\"\"Options dropped for exclusive mode.\n\n    before_pass: only exclusions earned by completed passes strictly below this\n    pass may apply. Keeps a pass's own results from shrinking the remaining slots\n    of the same pass (e.g. F1's pass-1 result must not drop options from F2's\n    pass-1 group — every factor gets full option coverage in its first pass).\n    \"\"\"\n    if not exclusive_mode or not all_option_ids:\n        return set()\n    gs = _group_dicts(groups)\n    if before_pass is not None:\n        gs = [g for g in gs if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) < before_pass]\n    n = len(all_option_ids)\n    max_exclude = min(n - MIN_OPTIONS_AFTER_EXCLUSION, int(math.floor(n * BOTTOM_PCT_CAP)))\n    if max_exclude <= 0:\n        return set()\n\n    completed_pass = max_completed_pass(gs)\n    if completed_pass < 1:\n        return set()\n\n    # Cumulative exclusion: each completed pass may add bottom 15% of original n\n    excluded: set[int] = set()\n    per_pass = max(1, int(math.floor(n * BOTTOM_PCT_PER_PASS))) if n >= 3 else 0\n    if per_pass <= 0:\n        return set()\n\n    remaining = list(all_option_ids)\n    for p in range(1, completed_pass + 1):\n        if len(excluded) >= max_exclude:\n            break\n        ranks = participant_option_ranks_for_pass(gs, pass_index=p, option_ids=remaining)\n        # worst first\n        ordered = sorted(remaining, key=lambda i: (-ranks.get(i, 0.0), i))\n        budget = min(per_pass, max_exclude - len(excluded), max(0, len(remaining) - MIN_OPTIONS_AFTER_EXCLUSION))\n        if budget <= 0:\n            break\n        drop = ordered[:budget]\n        for d in drop:\n            excluded.add(d)\n        remaining = [i for i in remaining if i not in excluded]\n    return excluded\n\n\ndef estimate_pass_comparisons(\n    option_n: int,\n    factor_n: int,\n    option_question_budget: int | None = None,\n    factor_question_budget: int | None = None,\n) -> int:\n    opt_stored = clamp_questions_per_group_stored(option_question_budget)\n    fac_stored = clamp_questions_per_group_stored(factor_question_budget)\n    total = 0\n    channels = factor_n if factor_n >= 1 else 1\n    if option_n >= 2:\n        opt_q = clamp_questions_per_group(opt_stored, option_n)\n        batches = option_batch_count(option_n, opt_q)\n        total += channels * batches * opt_q\n    if factor_n >= 2:\n        total += clamp_questions_per_group(fac_stored, factor_n)\n    return total\n\n\ndef build_progress(\n    *,\n    project: Any,\n    groups: list | None,\n    option_ids: list[int],\n    factor_ids: list[int],\n    exclusive_mode: bool,\n    comparisons_done: int | None = None,\n    groups_done: int | None = None,\n    in_group_total: int = 0,\n    in_group_done: int = 0,\n    seed: Any = None,\n) -> dict:\n    ps = project_pass_settings(project)\n    done_groups = _group_dicts(groups)\n    g_done = groups_done if groups_done is not None else len(done_groups)\n    c_done = comparisons_done if comparisons_done is not None else sum(\n        int(g.get(\"comparison_count\") or len(g.get(\"pairings\") or []) or 0) for g in done_groups\n    )\n    completed_pass = max_completed_pass(done_groups)\n    current_pass = completed_pass\n    all_opts = list(option_ids)\n    active_opts = list(all_opts)\n    factor_n = len(factor_ids)\n    opt_n = max(0, len(active_opts))\n    opt_budget, fac_budget = resolved_question_budgets(project, opt_n, factor_n)\n\n    def _remaining_slots(pass_index: int) -> list[dict]:\n        slots = required_slots_for_pass(\n            pass_index=pass_index,\n            alternative_ids=active_opts,\n            factor_ids=factor_ids,\n            option_question_budget=opt_budget,\n            factor_question_budget=fac_budget,\n            seed=seed,\n        )\n        return [\n            s for s in slots\n            if not (set(s[\"item_ids\"]) <= channel_covered_item_ids(\n                done_groups,\n                pass_index=pass_index,\n                group_type=s[\"group_type\"],\n                criterion_id=s[\"criterion_id\"],\n            ))\n        ]\n\n    if completed_pass == 0:\n        current_pass = 1\n    else:\n        remaining = _remaining_slots(completed_pass)\n        current_pass = completed_pass if remaining else completed_pass + 1\n\n    def comps_for_future_pass(p: int) -> int:\n        return estimate_pass_comparisons(opt_n, factor_n, opt_budget, fac_budget)\n\n    # remaining in current pass\n    remaining_slots = _remaining_slots(current_pass)\n    remaining_comps = 0\n    for s in remaining_slots:\n        remaining_comps += int(s.get(\"question_budget\") or estimated_comparisons(len(s[\"item_ids\"])))\n\n    target_max = ps[\"max_recommended_passes\"]\n    target_min = ps[\"min_expected_passes\"]\n    hard = ps[\"hard_max_passes\"]\n\n    def comps_done_through_pass(end_pass: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Actual comparisons recorded in completed groups up to end_pass inclusive.\"\"\"\n        total = 0\n        for g in done_groups:\n            if int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1) <= end_pass:\n                total += int(g.get(\"comparison_count\") or len(g.get(\"pairings\") or []) or 0)\n        return total\n\n    def total_to_pass(end_pass: int) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Estimate total comparisons from session start through end of end_pass.\"\"\"\n        if end_pass < 1:\n            return 0\n        # Already past this target: freeze at work actually done through that pass\n        # (do not inflate with optional later-pass remaining work).\n        if current_pass > end_pass:\n            return comps_done_through_pass(end_pass)\n        # Still working toward end_pass: done so far + rest of current + future full passes\n        t = c_done + remaining_comps\n        for p in range(current_pass + 1, end_pass + 1):\n            t += comps_for_future_pass(p)\n        return t\n\n    est_at_min = total_to_pass(target_min)\n    est_at_max = total_to_pass(target_max)\n    est_total = est_at_max  # primary overall bar targets recommended max\n\n    # When still short of a target, keep estimate >= done so the bar never exceeds 100%\n    # early if the participant overshot the algorithm upper bound. Once past the target\n    # pass, leave the frozen total as-is so the UI can stay full on optional work.\n    if current_pass <= target_min:\n        est_at_min = max(est_at_min, c_done)\n    if current_pass <= target_max:\n        est_at_max = max(est_at_max, c_done)\n        est_total = max(est_total, c_done)\n\n    return {\n        \"participant_comparisons_done\": c_done,\n        \"participant_groups_done\": g_done,\n        \"pass_index\": current_pass,\n        \"passes_completed\": completed_pass if not remaining_slots and completed_pass == current_pass else max(0, current_pass - 1) if remaining_slots else completed_pass,\n        \"min_expected_passes\": target_min,\n        \"max_recommended_passes\": target_max,\n        \"hard_max_passes\": hard,\n        \"estimated_comparisons_total\": est_total,\n        \"estimated_comparisons_at_min\": est_at_min,\n        \"estimated_comparisons_at_max\": est_at_max,\n        \"in_group\": {\"done\": in_group_done, \"total\": in_group_total},\n        \"ranking_evidence\": ranking_evidence_from_groups(done_groups),\n        \"ranking_option_ids\": [int(x) for x in all_opts],\n        \"ranking_factor_ids\": [int(x) for x in factor_ids],\n    }\n\n\ndef ranking_evidence_from_groups(groups: list | None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Compact per-group pairing snapshot for this participant's compare-session confidence bar.\"\"\"\n    out: list[dict] = []\n    for g in _group_dicts(groups):\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        rt = g.get(\"ranking_target\")\n        if hasattr(rt, \"value\"):\n            rt = rt.value\n        item_ids = [int(x) for x in (g.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or g.get(\"item_ids\") or []) if x is not None]\n        pairings: list[dict] = []\n        for p in (g.get(\"pairings\") or []):\n            if not isinstance(p, dict):\n                continue\n            row: dict[str, Any] = {\"response\": str(getattr(p.get(\"response\"), \"value\", p.get(\"response\") or \"winner\"))}\n            for k in (\"winner_id\", \"loser_id\", \"item_a_id\", \"item_b_id\", \"presented_left_id\", \"presented_right_id\"):\n                v = p.get(k)\n                if v is None:\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    row[k] = int(v)\n                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                    continue\n            pairings.append(row)\n        token = str(g.get(\"group_token\") or g.get(\"client_group_id\") or \"\")\n        out.append({\n            \"group_type\": str(gt or \"alternative\"),\n            \"criterion_id\": g.get(\"criterion_id\"),\n            \"pass_index\": int(g.get(\"pass_index\") or 1),\n            \"item_ids\": item_ids,\n            \"pairings\": pairings,\n            \"question_budget\": int(\n                g.get(\"requested_pairing_count\") or g.get(\"question_budget\") or g.get(\"estimated_comparisons\") or 0\n            ),\n            \"ranking_target\": str(rt or \"full\"),\n            \"top_n\": g.get(\"top_n\"),\n            \"client_group_id\": token or None,\n        })\n    return out\n\n\ndef historical_pairings_for_channel(\n    groups: list | None,\n    *,\n    group_type: str,\n    criterion_id: int | None,\n) -> list[dict]:\n    out: list[dict] = []\n    for g in _complete_group_dicts(groups):\n        gt = g.get(\"group_type\")\n        if hasattr(gt, \"value\"):\n            gt = gt.value\n        if str(gt) != str(group_type):\n            continue\n        if g.get(\"criterion_id\") != criterion_id:\n            continue\n        for p in (g.get(\"pairings\") or []):\n            if isinstance(p, dict):\n                out.append(normalize_pairing(p))\n    return out\n\n\ndef serialize_issued_group(\n    *,\n    project: Any,\n    slot: dict,\n    items: list[dict],\n    criterion: dict | None,\n    prior_pairings: list[dict],\n    existing: dict | None = None,\n) -> dict:\n    item_ids = [int(it[\"id\"]) for it in items]\n    n = len(item_ids)\n    target, top_n = resolve_ranking_target(project, n_items=n, group_type=slot[\"group_type\"])\n    budget = int(slot.get(\"question_budget\") or resolve_questions_per_group(\n        getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group\", None)\n        if slot[\"group_type\"] != \"criteria\"\n        else getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group\", None),\n        n,\n        bool(getattr(project, \"option_questions_per_group_explicit\", False))\n        if slot[\"group_type\"] != \"criteria\"\n        else bool(getattr(project, \"factor_questions_per_group_explicit\", False)),\n    ))\n    token = str((existing or {}).get(\"group_token\") or (existing or {}).get(\"client_group_id\") or new_group_token())\n    pairings = list((existing or {}).get(\"pairings\") or [])\n    return {\n        \"client_group_id\": token,\n        \"group_token\": token,\n        \"pass_index\": int(slot[\"pass_index\"]),\n        \"group_type\": slot[\"group_type\"],\n        \"criterion_id\": slot[\"criterion_id\"],\n        \"criterion\": criterion,\n        \"sort_algorithm\": select_sort_algorithm(n),\n        \"items\": items,\n        \"item_ids\": item_ids,\n        \"n_items\": n,\n        \"batch_index\": int(slot.get(\"batch_index\") or 0),\n        \"question_budget\": budget,\n        \"estimated_comparisons\": budget,\n        \"ranking_target\": target,\n        \"top_n\": top_n,\n        \"require_coverage\": int(slot[\"pass_index\"]) == 1,\n        \"prior_pairings\": prior_pairings,\n        \"pairings\": pairings,\n        \"algorithm_version\": str((existing or {}).get(\"algorithm_version\") or ALGORITHM_VERSION),\n        \"status\": str((existing or {}).get(\"status\") or \"in_progress\"),\n    }\n\n\ndef _slot_items_and_criterion(slot: dict, alt_by_id: dict, fac_by_id: dict, factor_ids: list[int]):\n    item_ids = list(slot[\"item_ids\"])\n    if slot[\"group_type\"] == \"criteria\":\n        items = [serialize_item(fac_by_id[i]) for i in item_ids if i in fac_by_id]\n        return items, None\n    items = [serialize_item(alt_by_id[i]) for i in item_ids if i in alt_by_id]\n    cid = slot[\"criterion_id\"]\n    if cid is not None and cid in fac_by_id:\n        criterion = serialize_criterion(fac_by_id[cid])\n    elif cid is None and not factor_ids:\n        criterion = {\n            \"id\": OVERALL_CRITERION_ID,\n            \"title\": \"Overall\",\n            \"description\": None,\n            \"comparison_question\": None,\n        }\n    else:\n        criterion = serialize_criterion(fac_by_id[cid]) if cid in fac_by_id else None\n    return items, criterion\n\n\ndef pick_next_sort_group(\n    *,\n    project: Any,\n    alternatives: list | None,\n    factors: list | None,\n    groups: list | None,\n    exclusive_mode: bool | None = None,\n    rng: random.Random | None = None,\n    max_pass_index: int | None = None,\n    seed: Any = None,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Return {group, progress, session_complete} for the next compare group.\n    group is None when no more groups (hard max or nothing left).\n    \"\"\"\n    rng = rng or random.Random()\n    excl_mode = bool(project.project_exclusive_mode if exclusive_mode is None else exclusive_mode)\n    alts = _active_items(alternatives)\n    facs = _active_items(factors)\n    alt_by_id = {_item_id(a): a for a in alts if _item_id(a)}\n    fac_by_id = {_item_id(f): f for f in facs if _item_id(f)}\n    all_option_ids = list(alt_by_id.keys())\n    factor_ids = list(fac_by_id.keys())\n    done = _group_dicts(groups)\n    ps = project_pass_settings(project)\n\n    progress = build_progress(\n        project=project,\n        groups=done,\n        option_ids=all_option_ids,\n        factor_ids=factor_ids,\n        exclusive_mode=excl_mode,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n\n    if not all_option_ids:\n        return {\"group\": None, \"progress\": progress, \"session_complete\": True, \"failure_reason\": \"No options to compare\"}\n\n    in_prog = _in_progress_group_dicts(done)\n    if in_prog:\n        g0 = in_prog[0]\n        slot = {\n            \"group_type\": (g0.get(\"group_type\").value if hasattr(g0.get(\"group_type\"), \"value\") else g0.get(\"group_type\") or \"alternative\"),\n            \"criterion_id\": g0.get(\"criterion_id\"),\n            \"pass_index\": int(g0.get(\"pass_index\") or 1),\n            \"batch_index\": int(g0.get(\"batch_index\") or 0),\n            \"item_ids\": list(g0.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or g0.get(\"item_ids\") or []),\n            \"question_budget\": int(g0.get(\"requested_pairing_count\") or 0),\n        }\n        items, criterion = _slot_items_and_criterion(slot, alt_by_id, fac_by_id, factor_ids)\n        prior = historical_pairings_for_channel(\n            done, group_type=slot[\"group_type\"], criterion_id=slot[\"criterion_id\"]\n        )\n        group = serialize_issued_group(\n            project=project, slot=slot, items=items, criterion=criterion,\n            prior_pairings=prior, existing=g0,\n        )\n        progress = build_progress(\n            project=project,\n            groups=done,\n            option_ids=all_option_ids,\n            factor_ids=factor_ids,\n            exclusive_mode=excl_mode,\n            in_group_total=group[\"question_budget\"],\n            in_group_done=len(group.get(\"pairings\") or []),\n            seed=seed,\n        )\n        progress[\"pass_index\"] = slot[\"pass_index\"]\n        return {\"group\": group, \"progress\": progress, \"session_complete\": False}\n\n    opt_budget, fac_budget = resolved_question_budgets(\n        project, len(all_option_ids), len(factor_ids)\n    )\n    pass_cap = int(ps[\"hard_max_passes\"])\n    if max_pass_index is not None:\n        try:\n            pass_cap = max(1, min(pass_cap, int(max_pass_index)))\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n            pass_cap = int(ps[\"hard_max_passes\"])\n\n    for pass_index in range(1, pass_cap + 1):\n        slots = required_slots_for_pass(\n            pass_index=pass_index,\n            alternative_ids=all_option_ids,\n            factor_ids=factor_ids,\n            option_question_budget=opt_budget,\n            factor_question_budget=fac_budget,\n            seed=seed,\n        )\n        opt_slots = [s for s in slots if s[\"group_type\"] == \"alternative\"]\n        crit_slots = [s for s in slots if s[\"group_type\"] == \"criteria\"]\n        rng.shuffle(opt_slots)\n        # Full-size groups first; remainder tails surface at the end of the pass.\n        opt_slots.sort(key=lambda s: -len(s[\"item_ids\"]))\n        ordered = opt_slots + crit_slots\n        for slot in ordered:\n            covered = channel_covered_item_ids(\n                done,\n                pass_index=pass_index,\n                group_type=slot[\"group_type\"],\n                criterion_id=slot[\"criterion_id\"],\n            )\n            if set(slot[\"item_ids\"]) <= covered:\n                continue\n            item_ids = list(slot[\"item_ids\"])\n            if len(item_ids) < 2:\n                continue\n            items, criterion = _slot_items_and_criterion(slot, alt_by_id, fac_by_id, factor_ids)\n            prior = historical_pairings_for_channel(\n                done, group_type=slot[\"group_type\"], criterion_id=slot[\"criterion_id\"]\n            )\n            group = serialize_issued_group(\n                project=project, slot=slot, items=items, criterion=criterion,\n                prior_pairings=prior,\n            )\n            progress = build_progress(\n                project=project,\n                groups=done,\n                option_ids=all_option_ids,\n                factor_ids=factor_ids,\n                exclusive_mode=excl_mode,\n                in_group_total=group[\"question_budget\"],\n                in_group_done=0,\n                seed=seed,\n            )\n            progress[\"pass_index\"] = pass_index\n            return {\"group\": group, \"progress\": progress, \"session_complete\": False}\n\n        if pass_index >= pass_cap:\n            break\n\n    progress = build_progress(\n        project=project,\n        groups=done,\n        option_ids=all_option_ids,\n        factor_ids=factor_ids,\n        exclusive_mode=excl_mode,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    return {\"group\": None, \"progress\": progress, \"session_complete\": True}\n\n\ndef package_group_id(package: dict | None) -> str:\n    if not package:\n        return \"\"\n    return str(package.get(\"group_token\") or package.get(\"client_group_id\") or \"\").strip()\n\n\ndef validate_group_package(\n    issued_group: dict | None,\n    package: dict,\n    *,\n    require_rank_order: bool = True,\n) -> str | None:\n    \"\"\"Return failure reason or None if ok.\"\"\"\n    if not package:\n        return \"Group package required\"\n    client_id = package_group_id(package)\n    if not client_id:\n        return \"group_token is required\"\n    issued_token = package_group_id(issued_group) if issued_group else \"\"\n    if issued_group and issued_token and issued_token != client_id:\n        issued_ids = [int(x) for x in (issued_group.get(\"item_ids\") or issued_group.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or [])]\n        initial = [int(x) for x in (package.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or package.get(\"item_ids\") or [])]\n        if sorted(issued_ids) != sorted(initial):\n            return \"Group does not match the issued compare group\"\n    initial = [int(x) for x in (package.get(\"item_ids_initial\") or package.get(\"item_ids\") or [])]\n    order = [int(x) for x in (package.get(\"rank_order\") or [])]\n    if require_rank_order:\n        if not order:\n            return \"rank_order is required\"\n        if sorted(order) != sorted(initial):\n            return \"rank_order must be a permutation of the group's items\"\n    allowed = set(order or initial)\n    pairings = package.get(\"pairings\") or []\n    if not isinstance(pairings, list):\n        return \"pairings must be a list\"\n    for p in pairings:\n        if not isinstance(p, dict):\n            return \"invalid pairing entry\"\n        w = p.get(\"winner_id\")\n        l = p.get(\"loser_id\")\n        a = p.get(\"item_a_id\")\n        b = p.get(\"item_b_id\")\n        ids = [x for x in (w, l, a, b) if x is not None]\n        if w is None or l is None:\n            if a is None or b is None:\n                return \"each pairing needs a pair of item ids\"\n        if allowed:\n            for x in ids:\n                if int(x) not in allowed:\n                    return \"pairing ids must be in the group\"\n    return None\n\n\ndef normalize_pairing(p: dict) -> dict:\n    resp = p.get(\"response\") or \"winner\"\n    if first item is better. RIGHT means the second item is better. \"\n            \"TIE only when they are genuinely even. except AiProviderError as ex:\n            if ex.retryable:\n                ut.show_vars_semi(\n                    f\"powerchoice_watcher - AI job {job.id} retrying\",\n state.answers.append(ans)\n        state.last_pair = nxt\n        job.pair_count = int(job.pair_count or 0) + 1\n        if response_kind == \"skipped\":\n            job.skip_count = {"type":"text","timestamp":1787339622043,"sessionID":"ses_fda463433ffe05Mbp697Cym8jx","part":{"id":"prt_025be3a8e001qv7qY9SwAZVUYC","messageID":"msg_025be318c0010QLnaaVQWaOkY9","sessionID":"ses_fda463433ffe05Mbp697Cym8jx","type":"text","text":"Build complete. Commit `6de6897` — `ai-agent-voting: fix SKIP/TIE threshold and decisive prompting`\n\n**Summary:**\n- Audited threshold logic: prior code had no prompt-mutation for SKIP/TIE and parsed SKIP as generic failure → 100% skipped. Fixed combined counting as `ceil(group_size*0.15)` and joint removal of both options.\n- Refined prompt decisiveness to bias away from SKIP while preserving abstention below threshold.\n\n**Major touch points:**\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:1` — added `import math`\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:6-37` — new helpers `ai_vote_threshold(group_size)` (`math.ceil(n*0.15)`) and `combined_abstentions(answers)` counting `skipped/skip/tie/unsure`\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:39-117` — extended `pairwise_messages(..., allow_skip_tie=True)` with branching: `allow True` → `LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP` + decisive line `Based on information about the project, make the best recommended choice for the user` + `Use TIE and SKIP sparingly...`; `allow False` → `LEFT or RIGHT` + `Never reply TIE or SKIP. Choose the better option even if imperfect.` and `Answer LEFT or RIGHT.` vs `Answer LEFT, RIGHT, TIE or SKIP.`\n- `src/utils/ai/prompts.py:119-157` — `parse_pairwise_choice` now explicitly maps `SKIP/SKIPPED/UNSURE/PASS/ABSTAIN` → `skipped` (token and `startswith` checks)\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:1` — added `import math`\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:48` — import `ai_vote_threshold`\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:268-285` — per-pair threshold wiring: `group_size = budget or len(items)`, `threshold = ai_vote_threshold(group_size)`, `combined = count skipped/tie/unsure in state.answers`, `allow_skip_tie = combined < threshold`, passed to `pairwise_messages`\n- `src/utils/ai/worker.py:304-308` — explicit `elif choice == \"skipped\"` branch\n- `src/config/config_settings.py:13` — bump `VERSION 0.7.70 → 0.7.71`\n\n**Verification:**\n- Unit reproduction: `ceil(n*0.15)` correct for n=1..20; prompt snapshot shows `SKIP` present only when `allow=True` and decisive line always present; `parse_pairwise_choice(\"SKIP\") → skipped`; simulated worker loop: budget 7 threshold 2 allows 0,1 then blocks ≥2; small group n=4 threshold 1 blocks after 1 abstention.\n- `pytest src/tests/test_comparison_question.py` — 2 passed\n- `pytest src/tests/test_ai_agents.py` — 27 passed (including `test_all_skipped_group_fails_job`, `test_retryable_llm_error_does_not_skip`)\n","time":{"start":1787339618958,"end":1787339622038},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"msg_01a025be39917cd1975863d9c8a83bc2"}}}}
